						}

							instant_build = yes
						}

							type = industrial_complex
							level = 2
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}

						add_building_construction = {
							type = office_park
							level = 2
							instant_build = yes
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = SOV_new_socialists_formed_flag
